- **robotframework**: Opinionated Robot Framework best-practices guide for writing and maintaining RF tests, libraries, and resources. Covers RF 7+ syntax, test design, locator strategy, deprecation migration, parallel/flake handling, and library architecture. Use when editing .robot/.resource files, modernizing legacy RF syntax, or designing custom RF libraries. ([urielpappilon/robotframework-skill](https://github.com/urielpappilon/robotframework-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/robotframework))

- **rollback-revision**: Roll back by moving one or more Unit heads to bound prior revisions via cub unit update --restore, then hand off to release-publish. Use for "roll back this change", "revert the last release", "undo the ChangeSet", or "restore to the last applied revision". Not for a clearer one-field forward fix. ([confighub/confighub-skills](https://github.com/confighub/confighub-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/rollback-revision) · ★2)
